Coordinator verification: CONFIRMED — `maker_file_attachments.role` is a write-time snapshot (`role_for(name)` at the attach doors; nothing rewrites a row; `project_folders.rb:15-17` says so); every widening of `ROLE_BY_EXT` has reached stored rows by hand: 9 data migrations (2026-07-28 → 2026-09-08, 651 lines) + 9 tests (1,114 lines) carrying 111 hand-copied extension entries, seven of them restating ONE rule ("an `other` row whose extension now has a role takes it"); TWO widenings never got their row half — `nc`/`tap`/`ngc` joined `gcode` in 77c29bc3 (2026-07-31; `file_attachment.rb:71`) and the 24 `pcb` extensions in efb7455e (2026-09-06; `:172-176`) whose migration `SeedPcbFilingRules` seeds filing rules only (no attachment class) — no migration names `nc`/`tap`/`ngc` or re-derives a `pcb` role (grep verified); the electronics hero prefers `%w[pcb image model]` (`project_kind.rb:110-113`, 8973e8ee, 2026-09-07), so a project whose Gerbers were attached between 2026-07-31 and 2026-09-06 shows a photo or the first file instead of the board; the two EQUALITY pins (`seed_pcb_filing_rules_migration_test.rb:129-131`, `rederive_model_roles_migration_test.rb:109-111`) make `file_attachment.rb:167-171`'s "`.xnc` … a one-line edit here with no other consequence" false at HEAD (the one line turns a migration test red, and existing rows stay `other`).
- Current complexity / invalid state: reachable today — an attachment stored `other` while `role_for(name)` is `pcb` or `gcode` (27 extensions): wrong hero for electronics projects, `other` told to v1 clients. The rule "stored roles follow the vocabulary" has NO owner: it lives in N self-contained migrations, each re-deciding which rows count as derived and each coupling history to the present through its test; a widening that forgets it fails silently, and two have.
- Proposed representation: make `other` mean, explicitly, "nothing classified this name when the row was written" — the one stored state the vocabulary can still improve — and give `Maker::FileAttachment` ONE method (`classify_unclassified!(user)`, ~10 lines: `where(role: "other")` joined to `nodes`, grouped by `role_for(name)`, `update_all` per role, excluding `other`) run per user by the existing daily `Maker::AttachmentReaperJob` (DB-only, backend-neutral, per-user failure isolation already there) plus a rake task for an immediate run after a widening deploy. One query per user per day, zero writes in steady state. The recorded decision to STORE the role stands (the cover stays query-free per card); the two rejections of deriving it (§5) are respected. Limits stated: a RE-classification (the `lbrn2` shape, 1 of 9 events) still needs a migration; a removed extension is not demoted.
- Smallest scope: `file_attachment.rb` (the method); `attachment_reaper_job.rb` (one per-user loop copying `reap_all!`'s rescue — or its own `config/recurring.yml` entry if the reaper's charter stays "dead nodes only"; owner's call); optional `lib/tasks` `maker:classify_roles`; relax the two EQUALITY pins to the per-key form the other seven use (S23's files); prose `files-preview.md:72-75,490-498`, `project_folders.rb:15-17`.
- Risks: the first run rewrites stale `other` rows to their vocabulary role (the same overwrite all seven from-`other` migrations performed); derived covers of affected projects/models may change (the intent); v1 `role` becomes more accurate, shape unchanged; up to 24 h between a widening deploy and rows following (the rake task covers it); no schema change, nothing destroyed; DB-only and identical on all three legs.
- Validation: existing — `file_attachment_test.rb` role pins, `project_kind_test.rb:568-573`, `importer_test.rb:106-114`, the reaper tests, the 9 migration tests (unchanged except the two pins). NEW, derived from the table rather than copied — for every `(ext, role)` in `ROLE_BY_EXT` an `other` row on `f.<ext>` becomes `role`; a deliberate non-`other` row is untouched; a row whose node is missing is untouched; a second run returns 0. Mutations: drop `where(role: "other")` → the deliberate-role case goes red; `.except("other", "pcb")` → the pcb sweep goes red; remove the job wiring → the job test (a stale `other` `.gbr` row → `pcb`) goes red. End to end: an electronics project with an `other` Gerber and a photo shows the photo before the sweep and the board after; skip the sweep → red.
- Confidence: high on the defect (git history, migration grep and reader trace agree — coordinator re-verified the dates, the grep and the pins), medium-high on the remedy (hosting it in the reaper or on its own schedule is the owner's call). Blast radius: one model + one job (+ two S23 test pins). Effort: small. Priority: P2 (defect-first; retires the per-widening migration cost). Coordination: S06 (files), S23 (migration tests), S15 (`HERO_ROLES` consumer).

### F-S33-2 — The filing map stores each user's DEVIATIONS from a code-derived default, not a per-user materialised copy of the vocabulary (no writes on read, no top-up migration per widening, no partial-map or reseed-gap states)
- Authoritative row: S33 (the files are S06's). Report: audit-2026-10-19-simplification/reports/S33.md, Opportunity 2. Coordinator verification: CONFIRMED — `FilingRules.seed!` inserts one row per `ROLE_BY_EXT` key + `BEYOND_ROLE_BY_EXT` (128 at HEAD) and `for` reads only those rows (`filing_rules.rb:46-66`); the copy is written on READ — by the sweep's first `FilingRules.for` and by a GET (`settings/filing_rules_controller.rb:19` calls `seed!`), while the hub must not seed and counts raw rows (`settings_controller.rb:1-12`), so a never-seeded user reads "0 rules" while 128 defaults apply; the `exists?` guard admits two states it cannot handle (an emptied map re-seeds — "an accepted gap", `:28-37`; a user holding one row is never completed); each widening needs a top-up migration because the guard forbids topping up on read — 4 of 4 widenings since the table was created (2026-09-04), each carrying a copy of the new keys and an equality-pinned test; the default derivation is spelled twice (`seed!` inlines `ROLE_FOLDERS.fetch`; `default_folder_for`, `project_folders.rb:102-104`, is documented as seed!'s helper and has NO production caller — grep verified).
- Current complexity / invalid state: storage cannot tell "the vocabulary's default" from "the user's choice", so (a) a new key cannot reach existing users without a migration; (b) "removed" is encoded as absence, which is also how "never seeded" and "newer than my seed" look — hence the guard, the reseed gap and the partial map; (c) reading must write (a GET seeds), and the one reader that must not write (the hub) disagrees with the one that does.
- Proposed representation: derive the default once at load (`DEFAULT = ROLE_BY_EXT.transform_values { ROLE_FOLDERS.fetch(_1) }.merge(BEYOND_ROLE_BY_EXT)`, raising at boot on an unmapped role) and store only deviations, with `folder: nil` meaning "I removed this default": `for(user) = DEFAULT.merge(user rows).compact`. Removed: `seed!`, the `exists?` guard, every write on read. A new key reaches every user with no migration; a removed default is a row, so it can never be resurrected (the /settings/filing promise holds by construction); the partial map and the reseed gap become unrepresentable; `default_folder_for` becomes `DEFAULT.fetch(ext, "other")` or goes. The invariant's REASON (never overrule an edit, never resurrect a removal; `filing_rules.rb:2-7`) is kept; only its mechanism ("by migration, once") is retired.
- Smallest scope: a migration (`change_column_null :maker_filing_rules, :folder, true` + a one-time conversion inserting `(ext, NULL)` for every HEAD default key a user with rows LACKS — the last 128-key literal — so no user's effective map changes on deploy; `down` deletes the NULL rows); `filing_rules.rb` (`DEFAULT`, new `for`, delete `seed!`); `filing_rule.rb` (nil folder only for a `DEFAULT` key); `settings/filing_rules_controller.rb` + routes (`param: :ext`) + `_folder_card.html.erb` (index groups `for`; destroy writes `(ext, nil)` for a default key / deletes a custom row; create keeps the Remove-then-Add UX; update upserts); `settings_controller.rb` (count = `for(current_user).size`); `project_folders.rb`; prose `project-intake.md:235-249`, `files-preview.md:20-31,490-498`, the three file headers.
- Risks: defaults become LIVE for keys a user has not touched — for additive widenings that is the point (what each top-up migration did by hand), but a future RE-classification or `ROLE_FOLDERS` rename would now reach untouched users, where today it reaches only users seeded after it: the author of such a change must decide that explicitly (the OWNER should accept this trade before the slice is cut); converted users are unaffected (existing rows stay as deviations); the conversion MUST record removals or a removed rule is resurrected (new test 4 pins it); on SQLite `change_column_null` is a table copy (small table); chip actions keyed by ext instead of id, tenancy becomes structural; DB-only, identical on all three legs.
- Validation: existing — `filing_rules_test.rb` (rewrite the seed cases), `project_folders_test.rb:100-119`, `project_intake_test.rb`'s custom-rule case, `settings_filing_test.rb`, `settings_hub_test.rb:66`, `page_query_budget_test.rb:1266-1349` (still constant, without the pre-seed workaround). NEW with mutations: (1) a user with deviations still resolves an untouched default key (mutation: `for` reads rows only → red); (2) removing `.stl` leaves `folder_for(user, "x.stl") == "other"` and renders no chip (mutation: `destroy` deletes instead of writing nil → `.stl` returns to `models` → red); (3) GET /settings/filing, GET /settings and `FilingRules.for` write zero rows (mutation: re-add a seed call → red); (4) a user missing `.stl` before the conversion still files `.stl` into `other` after it (mutation: skip the NULL inserts → red).
- Confidence: medium (mechanism small and fully in the code; it rewrites the settings routes and makes future default changes live — both choices for the owner). Blast radius: one service + one model + one controller/views + one migration. Effort: medium. Priority: P3 (owner's acceptance first). Coordination: S06 (files), S23 (migration), S20 (settings view). Independent of F-S33-1.

### F-S34-1 — A thumbnail spinner turns only inside a poll cell: key the animation on `[data-thumbs-target~="cell"]` in `components.css` and delete `public.css`'s hand-kept list of containers that do not poll (the list already misses two public surfaces, and two private pickers mount a poller with no cells)
- Authoritative row: S34 (the component rule is S20's file). Report: audit-2026-10-19-simplification/reports/S34.md, Opportunity 1. Coordinator verification: CONFIRMED — `components.css:315-319` animates every `.thumb-spinner`; `pages/public.css:38-42` stops it for a hand-kept list (`.public-files`, `.public-photos`, `.public-tiles`) whose own comment records the list has grown once and is "the rule that is easiest to forget"; `thumbs_controller.js:15,31-39` defines pending as "a cell target holding a `.thumb-spinner`" and returns at once with no cells; two public `files/thumb` render sites are OUTSIDE the list — `public/projects/_build_log.html.erb:29-33` (`div.entry-photos`, since the commit that wrote the list) and `_block_panel.html.erb:50-52` (`a.block-thumb`, whose comment claims public.css covers it) — so a pending build-log photo or image-block thumbnail turns forever for a visitor (and on the owner's preview, which renders the same body); privately, `projects/_page_intro.html.erb:30-44` and `_block_editor.html.erb:52-66` mount `data-controller="thumbs"` over `button.image-pick` with no `data-thumbs-target="cell"` (grep verified), so `cellTargets` is empty and the pickers' spinners turn until reload — the opposite of f218c38a's commit message; four files (`files-preview.md:602-606`, `public_project.rb:425-428`, `_files.html.erb:14-18`, `_block_panel.html.erb:41-43`) promise the list covers every public spinner; no test can see any of it (`maker_page_thumbnails_test.rb:129` checks markup; `public_project_test.rb:464` asserts the controller is absent).
- Current complexity / invalid state: one fact — "will anything resolve this spinner?" — is decided by the markup (the cell target) and then written AGAIN by hand in a page sheet; every surface that renders `files/thumb` without a poll needs a second edit nothing ties to the first; the list knows only public containers (a private mount with no cell gets the same endless spinner with nowhere to record it). The invalid state: a spinner that animates, promising an answer, in a region no poll will ever visit — live on two public surfaces and two pickers.
- Proposed representation: the spinner reads the poll's own predicate — `.thumb-spinner` carries no animation; `[data-thumbs-target~="cell"] .thumb-spinner { animation: thumb-spin … }` in `components.css` (the repo's own rules point here: `preview.css:85-87` "styled off `data-group` and `data-state`, never off classes of their own"; `components.css:327-331` keeps `.thumb-failed` in components as "a state of the component"); delete `public.css:35-42`. A new public partial or a mount that forgets its cell wiring then shows an honest still ring with no edit anywhere else; adding the wiring is what makes it turn — and what makes the thumbnail resolve.
- Smallest scope: `components.css:315-319` (move `animation` to the cell-scoped selector — S20's file); delete `pages/public.css:35-42`; update the four prose sites; tests below. No markup, controller, route or addon change. The pickers' REAL fix is S15's (add `thumb_cell_data(row.node_id, ext)` to `button.image-pick` so they poll); with this rule in place that change also brings their spin back.
- Risks: the two uncovered public sites and the two pickers become still rings (the intended state); all 17 private `files/thumb` sites checked one by one (15 sit inside a cell under a thumbs controller); the CSS now names a Stimulus target — renaming it stops every spinner, a safe failure the new private-side test catches; addons unaffected (no bundle renders `.thumb-spinner`; not one of the 52 contract classes); reduced-motion `!important` unchanged; CSS only, identical on all legs.
- Validation: existing — `maker_page_thumbnails_test.rb:129`, `public_project_test.rb:464`, `shell_test.rb:214`. NEW system test, both directions: a published project with a build-log photo and an image block whose nodes have `pending` rows → on the public page every `.thumb-spinner` has `getComputedStyle(el).animationName == "none"` (RED today for `.entry-photos` and `.block-thumb`); on the Library grid with a pending row → `"thumb-spin"`. Mutation 1: put `animation` back on the bare `.thumb-spinner` → the public half goes red. Mutation 2: drop the cell-scoped rule → the private half goes red. Optional `css_hygiene_test` guard: no page sheet sets `animation` on `.thumb-spinner` (mutation: restore `public.css:41-42` → red).
- Confidence: high (both public misses and the pickers' empty `cellTargets` traced from markup and controller; the cascade is ordinary; not observed in a browser). Blast radius: two stylesheets + four prose sites. Effort: trivial. Priority: P2 (defect-first, cheap). Coordination: S20 (`components.css`), S15 (the pickers' cell wiring), S14 (the public prose).

### F-S34-2 — The `hidden` attribute is authoritative ONCE, in `@layer base` (`[hidden]:not([hidden="until-found"]) { display: none !important; }`), instead of being restated per component — seven restatements in three sheets, each added after a shipped bug, and an eighth case missing today (the page editor's YAML drawer renders while `hidden`)
- Authoritative row: S34, co-owned with S20 (the base rule's home and four of the seven selectors are S20's files). Report: audit-2026-10-19-simplification/reports/S34.md, Opportunity 2. Coordinator verification: CONFIRMED — any author `display` beats the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }`, so the attribute works only where a component restates it; the restatements are `tools.css:71,97` (3 selectors; two belt-and-braces since `li` and `.box-group` set no `display`), `components.css:220,453,558` and `shell.css:117` (grep verified: exactly these seven selectors across all sheets, each with a comment telling the bug story — the hidden-flash "Problem" box on every openscad load, the file chooser's Back button, the tab panes, the top-bar caption); `components.css:181` declares `.layout-drawer { … display: flex … }`, `projects/_layout_drawer.html.erb:8` renders `<aside class="layout-drawer" hidden …>` ("Hidden until 'Edit as YAML'"), `layout_editor_controller.js:430-436` toggles the attribute, and NO `.layout-drawer[hidden]` rule exists (grep verified) — so from the moment edit mode opens the drawer (Copy, Apply, an empty textarea) renders below the panel grid and "closing" it leaves it on screen; the tests check only the attribute (`project_layout_editor_test.rb:48`, `page_editor_test.rb:146-147`); `file_chooser.js:237-238` calls `el.hidden` "the house idiom … the one the app's own reset already honours" — `git log -S'[hidden]'` shows no such reset ever existed; the addon bundles already work around it ("REMOVED RATHER THAN `hidden`, WHICH IS DECORATIVE ON THIS ELEMENT", tinkerex-addons `boxes/editor.js:170-177`); ~290 selectors set a visible `display`, 7 restate `[hidden]`.
- Current complexity / invalid state: one bit has two owners — the attribute everything writes, and each component's `display`, which wins by default; correctness depends on a per-component restatement discovered only after a shipped bug (four recorded; the drawer is a fifth nobody has found); the addon bundles can use `hidden` only on the classes this repo happens to restate. Invalid states: an element with `hidden` that is visible and focusable (the drawer today); a control marked `aria-expanded="false"` beside a region that is visible.
- Proposed representation: one rule in `application.css`'s base layer — `!important` in an EARLY layer is the cascade's own tool for this (important declarations reverse layer order, so it beats every component, shell, page and vendor rule); `until-found` keeps its UA behaviour. Delete the seven restatements (optional once inert, but what leaves ONE definition). "Hidden" has one definition; the drawer is fixed with no edit to components.css; every addon-shared class honours `hidden`. Not F-S20-1's pattern re-found: that is one rule block duplicated across two triggers; this is markup state a CSS declaration silently overrides. The obvious alternative — a seventh restatement `.layout-drawer[hidden]` — is the pattern that produced the defect.
- Smallest scope: `application.css` base layer (one rule; S20's file); delete `tools.css:71,97` (this row's); delete `components.css:215-220,450-453,553-558` and `shell.css:115-117` with their comments (S20's); `design-system.md` "The cascade": one sentence (hidden is authoritative in base; components never restate it); tests below.
- Risks: CSS that deliberately SHOWS an element carrying `hidden` would now hide it — the eight `hidden` elements in five templates, the eight attribute writes in five JS files and every `hidden` in the addon bundles were checked: none relies on the override; the drawer is the only behaviour that changes, to what its own comment documents; Lexxy's `[data-dropdown-panel][hidden] { display: none }` agrees; `!important` is already established in base (`application.css:179`, reduced motion) and `css_hygiene_test` has no rule against it; addons: `hidden` becomes strictly more effective; data/API/legs unaffected.
- Validation: existing — once the restatements go, `shell_test.rb:161` (hidden-flash probe), `repo_browser_test.rb` ("no Back at its root"), the `page_tabs` suite, `system/topbar_upload_test.rb`, the box filter in `box_sections_test.rb` together prove the base rule covers each former restatement. NEW in `page_editor_test.rb`: `open_editor` then `assert_no_selector "aside.layout-drawer"` (Capybara counts visible elements; RED today); click "Edit as YAML" → `assert_selector`; click again → `assert_no_selector` (mutation: delete the base rule → red). NEW probe generalising `shell_test.rb:161`: inject `<button class="btn" hidden>`, `<aside class="layout-drawer" hidden>`, `<div class="addon-card" hidden>` → none visible (mutation: delete the base rule; with the restatements gone all three go red). NEW `css_hygiene_test` assertions: the base layer carries the rule, and no other sheet declares `[hidden] { display: none }` (mutations: delete the rule → red; re-add `.addon-card[hidden]` → red).
- Confidence: medium-high (the cascade behaviour is certain and this codebase has measured it three times; the drawer defect is inferred from it, not observed — observe in a browser first). Blast radius: four stylesheets + one invariants sentence. Effort: small. Priority: P2 (defect-first; closes a recurring bug class). Coordination: S20 (co-owner), S15 (the drawer page), S11 (the bundles' `hidden` use).

## 3. Explicit skip decisions
(rows recorded as completed coverage without a finding; a skip row carries Boundary + Status only — §1's four-line shape applies to review rows)

- S30 — Documentation (`docs/**`, `*.md`): skip — prose, not code; the invariants files are inputs to the rows that cite them.
- S31 — Vendored JS and static scaffold (incl. `app/assets/stylesheets/{jspreadsheet,jsuites}.css`, moved here 2026-10-19): skip — third-party artefacts, not ours to restructure.
- S35 — Per-subsystem test files reviewed as code: skip — the Tests lines are validation inventory; test code as code was reviewed at the harness (S27) and gate (S26) levels; the measured limit (117 cited tests never opened by the citing row) is stated in §1.
- S32 (addon fixture snapshot): reviewed → F-S32-1. S33 (vocabulary) → F-S33-1, F-S33-2. S34 (page stylesheets) → F-S34-1, F-S34-2. No batch-5 row skipped.

## 4. Cross-cutting patterns
(observed across rows; not themselves recommendations unless assigned to a row)

- (S01, S04, S11 reports) **Stale prose citing the retired embedded engine** (`Sync::`, `FileLayer::Chunk`, `Storage::ChunkStore`, `SYNC_ENGINE`, `engine_wanted?`): `file_layer.rb:1-6`, `capabilities.rb:53-65` (describes DIR_DELETABLE as live), `upload_begun.rb:11-17`, `upload_finalized.rb:11-27`, `library_broadcast.rb:3-35`, `chunk_remote.rb:4-8,389-397`, `transport.rb:13`, `remote_changelog_pull_job.rb:96-108`, `scan_job.rb:151-155`, `library_output.rb:119-132,141-213`, `addons_controller.rb:500-509` (a lock-ordering argument whose cost — `next_seq!` under `Sync::` — no longer exists), `addon_origins.rb:31-33` (claims `title` is not passed; line 46 passes it). Docs-only; not a simplification finding, but the lock-ordering comment hides a trade-off worth re-deciding (S11).
- RESOLVED 2026-10-19 → see F-S24-1 (DEMOTED; latency note below). (S01 report) **Three recurring jobs carry the inverse gate** `return if FileLayer.current.capabilities.scannable?` (`project_intake_job.rb:50`, `model_intake_job.rb:31`, `project_discovery_job.rb:55,81`): on chunk_remote three independent 15-minute schedules stand in for ScanJob's single sequenced post-reconcile run. Candidate for S05/S06/S07 to assess as one post-catalog sequence driven by both ScanJob and RemoteChangelogPullJob.
- (S04 report) **Defects, not simplifications, each worth a ticket**: zero-byte desktop-synced files unreadable on chunk_remote ("no rows" means both unmirrored and empty); cursor AHEAD of a restored server goes unnoticed; four socket errors unmapped to 503; `remote_manifest_chunks` append-only; Postgres-only concurrent-apply race; three dead/vacuous tests; unindexed `(user_id, current_version_id)` lookup in `finalize_upload`.
- (S11 report) **Guard gap**: `Tools::LibraryFolders#chosen_parent_node_id` lacks the `is_a?(String)` check its sibling `destination_from_param` has (a hand-built array param becomes an IN lookup on the write door). One-line fix. **Concurrency trade-off**: the addon save transaction spans sync-server HTTP calls on chunk_remote, holding SQLite's write lock.
- (S01 report) `Subtree::MAX_DEPTH` is now only a silent-subset cap (the `seen` filter already terminates cycles); a raise would match S3's `DIR_DELETE_MAX_OBJECTS` refusal shape. Below the bar; ticket-sized.

- (S10 report) **Defect, not a simplification**: `FilesController#rescan` (`files_controller.rb:1135`) rebuilds its redirect from `folder`/`ext` only while the button posts `files_query_base` (`index.html.erb:89`, which carries `q` and `selected`), so a rescan started from a search lands at the unfiltered root. One-line fix (`q: resolve_query`).
- (S10 report) The eight-branch viewer chain is spelled twice (`files/show.html.erb:109-182`, `public/files/view.html.erb:64-102`) — same divergence class as F-S10-2; the "ready-and-attached" preview predicate (`status == "ready" && asset.attached?`) repeats ~10x across private and public views (candidate for S08 to assess). OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19: S14 considered and rejected one shared chain partial; it contradicts the recorded per-page decision at `files/show.html.erb:103-108`.

- (S15 report) **Defects, one-line fixes**: `layout_editor_controller.js:249` counts tab slots against the 40-block cap (the server was fixed for exactly this at `layout_document.rb:361-364`); `project_kind.rb:445-450` `stored_views` accepts any VIEW for any key where the door uses `offered_views`; tab names read under TITLE_MAX=60 but written under TAB_NAME_MAX=30; `fetchYaml`/`setView` apply responses in arrival order (a late older response overwrites newer drawer text).
- (S15 report → S14) `Maker::PublicProject#thumbs` (`public_project.rb:429-431`) backfills `listing.node_ids + page_nodes.keys` — hidden folders and closed repos included — on an anonymous GET: a THIRD answer to "what does this page paint"; `#hidden_file_ids`/`#hidden_dir_ids`/`#flatten_dirs` re-walk the tree; `#repo_cards` repeats `ProjectsController#show`'s batched repo-root query + `RepoCard.for` loop (`public_project.rb:336-348` vs `projects_controller.rb:292-302`). Handed to S14's reviewer as pointers.
- (S15 report) `Maker::Project#visible_panels` is dead in app code (tests only) and its header still says show.html.erb loops over it.

- (S05 report → S03/S05, reasoned not probed) **An S3 listing is not a snapshot against concurrent web writes**: an object listed, then trashed by `S3#delete` before the Reconciler's `#live` pluck, is re-created as a ghost live node by `create_or_revive!` (share survives the same window because `hash_for` re-reads the bytes and gets ENOENT; S3 observations carry their identity from the listing). On a library small enough that one file exceeds the 20% guard, every later scan refuses. Handed to S03's reviewer. OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19: NOT ASSESSED by S03 — S03 was dispatched before S05 was harvested and its report never mentions it; owner S05 (`s3_observer.rb`, `reconciler.rb`). The mechanism reads true at HEAD (`s3_observer.rb:510` takes identity from the listing; `reconciler.rb:769-771`; `create_or_revive!` cannot match a NULL-path trash tombstone, `:536-537`): an object listed, then trashed in the web, becomes a NEW live node. Probe with FakeS3 (list → adapter delete → reconcile) before filing it as a defect; neither F-S02-1 nor F-S03-1 changes this path.
- (S05 report) Tombstones of both kinds are never destroyed on share/s3 (1,455 tombstones vs 3,830 live rows in the 2026-09-07 snapshot); every user_id-scoped scan walks them. Lifecycle owner unclear (S02/S03 trash vs S06 reapers). `Share#trashed`/`S3#trashed` also run as a user_id scan + temp B-tree; F-S05-1's index would serve them (plan-verified only).

- (S06 report) **Defect, in boundary, not a simplification — HTTP 500 on a reachable (if unoffered) path**: `Maker::AttachToProject#refusal_for` answers `:in_working_tree` (`attach_to_project.rb:192`) and `#relocate` returns it (`:148`), but `Projects::AttachmentsController#relocate`'s exhaustive `case` (`attachments_controller.rb:118-128`) has no arm for it → `else raise ArgumentError` → 500. Introduced by 2217257f; the UI never offers the button in that state (`move_conflicts.rb:89`), so it needs a crafted POST or a stale prompt. Fix: one `when :in_working_tree` arm + a test driving every `#refusal_for` symbol through `#relocate`.
- (S01 + S06 reports, CONVERGENT — RESOLVED 2026-10-19 → F-S07-1 (positional claims); the sequenced-sweep answer F-S24-1 was DEMOTED to the latency note below) **chunk_remote has no discover → file → catalogue SEQUENCE.** On share/s3 `Library::ScanJob#perform` runs `discover_projects` → `intake_project_folders` → `intake_models` inline (`scan_job.rb:96-98`) because the order changes the answer (`project-intake.md:134-146`, `model-intake.md:88-93,205-211`; `ModelIntake.claimed_folders` relies on it). On chunk_remote the three are independent 15-minute recurring entries (`config/recurring.yml:93-95,158-160,192-194`), each gated `return if FileLayer.current.capabilities.scannable?` (`project_intake_job.rb:50`, `project_discovery_job.rb:55,81`, `model_intake_job.rb:31`), chained only discovery→intake and only when discovery created something. A folder pushed under `Projects/` can be catalogued as a permanent auto folder model before discovery claims it (reasoned, not reproduced). The S06 reviewer also notes discovery's MAX_PROJECTS=50 cap leaves the same hole on share/s3. One candidate fix for both: `ModelIntake.claimed_folders` also treats discovery's adoptable candidates as claimed (S07's file); the structural alternative is one post-catalog sequence driven by both ScanJob and RemoteChangelogPullJob (S24/S05). Handed to S07's reviewer as a pointer.
- (S06 report → S15) The intake and the project page disagree about what is "in" a project folder: `ProjectIntake#subtree_files` (`:573-589`) attaches everything under every live dir (including `.git/**` on chunk_remote, dot-dirs, `node_modules` on every backend) while `ProjectFolderListing#collect_dirs` hides those; the extra rows are loaded for every project card's cover, returned by the v1 project JSON and re-read daily by the reaper. A product decision (share the listing's hidden-dir rule in the intake?), not a representation finding.
- (S06 report) Stale prose: `project_discovery_job.rb:11-15`, `scans_controller.rb:104-105`, `project-intake.md:228-231` say the `(user_id, folder_node_id)` unique index is missing/open work — it exists (`db/schema.rb:376`); `project-intake.md:327-330` says the §14d `port_writable?` count "stays five" (now one); `project_intake.rb:93-103,463-469` cost the chunk path in retired embedded-engine terms; `model_intake.rb:152-154` vs `project_intake.rb:631-632` contradict each other about `insert_all` timestamp stamping (S07 to confirm which is right). Dead code: `Maker::ProjectIntakeJob.nudge` has no production caller.
- (S06 report → S15/S07/S16) `has_many :file_attachments, -> { order(:position) }` on project/model/item/build_log_entry orders on a column that is 0 for every row the web writes, so association order is plan-dependent on Postgres; `order(:position, :id)` would make it deterministic.

- (S08 report) Stale prose: `docs/adding-a-preview-format.md:55-57` and `enqueue.rb:65-67` name `V1::CommitsController#finalize` as an enqueue seam (no such controller exists); `preview.rb:2` and `preview_test.rb:27` still say one row per (node, version, kind); `generate_preview_job.rb:12-13`/`registry.rb:9-10` say "Phase 1 registers ONLY SVG"; `inspector_test.rb:105-107` misstates which states make `current_thumbnail` and `Enqueue.call` differ. `extract_pdf_metadata_job.rb:33` reads a whole PDF under only the 256 MB port cap (vs the 32 MB preview ceiling) — a resource note. `public/files_controller.rb:108`'s `expires_in 1.minute` lets a browser keep a redirect to an old-build geometry payload for up to a minute after a rebuild.
- (S08 report) The "ready-and-attached" predicate repeated ~10x that S10 flagged is covered by F-S08-2's `showable?`/`current_build?` — superseded, see §5.

- (S03 report → S01/S10/S30) **A recorded invariant is false on one backend**: CLAUDE.md §14a's "a refusal is recoverable — restore the folder, then the file" and the Trash/help copy promise a folder restore S3 cannot perform; `adapter_mutation_contract.rb` has no folder-restore case (how the gap stayed invisible) and its header still describes `dir_deletable`, retired 2026-09-10. Whether chunk_remote's server brings a folder's contents back with it is not pinned here either (the journey restores the child with a second POST).
- (S03 report → S06, unverified) A project folder brought back by a leftover marker returns under a NEW node_id; whether `Maker::ProjectDiscovery` then treats it as unclaimed was not checked.
- (S03 report → S05) `TrashPurgeJob#purge_s3!` drives `adapter.client` directly (`trash_purge_job.rb:163,187`), a second owner of the S3 trash keyspace; it reads nothing past the node-id segment, so F-S03-1 does not affect it.

- (S09 report → S08/S10/S18) A `geometry` build can be requested for ANY extension — `FilesController#geometry` enqueues with no `MEASURABLE_EXTS` check and the v1 previews endpoint takes `kind` from the client — and `Registry.render` passes `kind:` only to renderers whose `render` declares it, so a renderer that ignores or lacks `kind` (stl, pdf, image_thumb, three_mf, gerber) stores a PNG under kind "geometry" and serves it `ready` to a JSON consumer. The geometry-capable set is spelled in ~6 places (`MEASURABLE_EXTS`, four renderers' arms, `Geometry::REVISIONED_BY`), held together by GeometryTest. One ext → builder table read by the job would let an unsupported kind fail closed as `unsupported`. Candidate for S08's owner; not a separate finding here. OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19: S08 was harvested before S09 and never assessed this; S33 (vocabulary row) did: `FilesController#geometry` (`:445-483`) enqueues `kind: "geometry"` for any present node with no `MEASURABLE_EXTS` check; v1 accepts any `Maker::Preview::KINDS` value (`previews_controller.rb:46-55`) — no renderer produces `render`, only Mesh refuses `model_glb`; `Registry.render` drops `kind:` for a renderer that does not declare it (`registry.rb:167-173`) and `Stl` ignores it, so such a row stores a PNG and answers `ready`. The shipped desktop client enqueues only `thumbnail`, so the path is hand-crafted requests only: a DOOR-SIDE guard (the web door checks `MEASURABLE_EXTS`; v1 validates `kind` against the renderer's declared kinds) closes it — a defect, not a representation change (S33 rejected the derived-dispatch rewrite as a relocation).
- (S09 report) Defects in boundary, not simplifications: the payload's `i` flag means "upright base64 PNG" on the LightBurn leg but "an `<image>` element" on the SVG leg, so a rotated or non-PNG LightBurn bitmap draws as a solid cut contour in Measure/print/export (fix needs a `LbrnGeometry::FORMAT_REVISION` bump); the paper preference reads/writes localStorage unguarded (the unit preference is guarded) — in blocked storage `populatePaper` throws inside `draw()`; `geometry_test.rb:94-95` misstates which DXF term bumped.
- (S09 report → S22) `modelviewer_controller.js:35-40` (lazy mode) does not rest the element on a Turbo snapshot restore, unlike the Gerber controller — the same hazard as F-S09-1's third path. Handed to S22's reviewer.
- (S09 report → S30) `docs/adding-a-preview-format.md:30-34` names `REVISIONED_BY` and the revision constants but not `MEASURABLE_EXTS`, the `render(kind:)`/`payload_revision` arms, `available_for?` or GeometryTest's fixture tables; the tests catch each omission, the walkthrough does not list them.

- (S02 report) **Defect, one-line fix**: `Share#write`'s `FileUtils.mkdir_p(dest.dirname)` (`share.rb:257`) sits OUTSIDE the `begin` at `:273`, so a failure to re-create a catalog folder missing on disk raises a raw Errno → 500 instead of FetchError → 503 — the exact bug class delete/move/undelete record fixing. (The post-rename `File.stat` calls at `:285,:349,:606,:707` are also unmapped; narrower.)
- (S02 report → S03/S05, product decision) Browser `write`/`mkdir` (`share.rb:294,:337`; `s3.rb:379,:413`) never revive a scan tombstone at the same path while the Reconciler does (`create_or_revive!`, `ensure_dirs`): a browser upload after a Finder deletion mints a new node_id where a Finder re-creation revives the old one; a project whose folder was removed in Finder is not re-linked when the folder is re-made in the browser. Whether a browser upload should inherit a Finder-deleted file's attachments is a product call; its clean form is one "node at rel" ladder shared with the Reconciler and S3.
- (S02 report → S01) `test/services/file_layer/subtree_test.rb:41-48` labels a fixture "tombstoned by a scan" but builds the TRASH shape (`storage_path: nil`); `trash_controller.rb:121-122` promises "its contents come back with it" — untrue for a scan tombstone converted by today's cascade (fixed by F-S02-1).

- (S14 report) **Defects, not simplifications**: the public `.gbrjob` page has an N+1 (`gerber_layers_for` → `layer_url_for` → `permits?` → `visible_entry?` is one `exists?` per layer on an anonymous GET; `page_query_budget_test.rb:499-527` claims one bounded query but its subject is an `.stl`) — prime visibility once for the sibling set as `visible_page_ids` does; **deliverable is wider than listable** — `claimed?` refuses only a `.git` lineage while the listing skips every dot-/build-output directory, so a plainly named file under `.github/`, `.vscode/`, `.pio/` or `node_modules/` inside a published folder is deliverable by node id (UUIDs, nothing links them) — narrowing it is the owner's call; the public page has no ROOT repo card (a project whose folder IS the working tree shows a README but no head commit/tags) — product question.
- (S14 report → S10) The public file page lets an ANONYMOUS visitor trigger F-S10-1's cache poisoning: `Share#stat` is catalog-only so `Public::FilesController#view` reaches `GerberArchive.for` on an unmounted share, and the cache key names no user or surface, so the poisoned nil is then served to the owner's private page too. Raises F-S10-1's priority.
- (S14 report) Stale prose: `project.rb:317` ("MINTED … NEVER CLEARED OR ROTATED" — rotated by UnlistedTokensController since S4-4); `public_project.rb:294-296`; `public_project_test.rb:550-553` ("never consult the layout" — tinkerex#283 gated all three).

- (S13 report) **Defects, not simplifications**: `editor_controller.js:103` parses JSON before checking `res.ok` with no catch — FilesController's class rescues answer HTML redirects to a `fetch`, so a node tombstoned between render and Save leaves the editor on "Saving…" (`history_controller.js:267` already uses `res.json().catch(...)`); `repo_commits_controller.rb:86-92` accepts any present dir node as `root_id`, the `.git` marker itself included (workdir `<repo>/.git` while libgit2 opens `<repo>` → every tracked path shows `:deleted` and committable; lock checks look in `.git/.git/`) — unprobed, crafted URL only; `FilesController#restore` (`:749-797`) compares the old blob against DISK, not HEAD, so it overwrites uncommitted working-tree edits without warning; `projects/readmes_controller.rb:43-49` has no `FileLayer::NotFound` arm and ApplicationController no `rescue_from`, so on chunk_remote a sync-server 404 inside the mirror's ~2-minute lag is a 500 (the hole `text_files_controller.rb:49-52` names for "every future door outside FilesController") — S15's file.
- (S13 report → S10) The file page's banner prints the `maker_repo_edits` row COUNT as "N pending change(s)" (`files/show.html.erb:128-131`, `files_controller.rb:530`), but rows are cleaned up only when the commit box opens or on commit; after a terminal commit the page says "pending" while the box says "Nothing to commit" (`repo_edit.rb:4-7` forbids reading a row as proof). Options: drop the number, or word it "edited in tinkerex".
- (S16 report) **Defect, outside the brief**: `Projects::EntryPhotosController#destroy` (`entry_photos_controller.rb:33-38`) finds ANY of the user's attachments and calls `att.attachable.project` — given a project/model/item attachment id it destroys the row then 500s (NoMethodError) after the delete committed; no view renders the route. Scope to `attachable_type: "Maker::BuildLogEntry"` or delete the dead route.
- (S16 report) `Task.status` + `completed_at` kept in sync by hand is ALREADY recorded as X6 / F-40 in `docs/AUDIT-2026-08-19.md:993-1019` (ACCEPTED, still open as a product decision) — not re-raised; three new facts for that record: the web create door permits `:status` but never stamps the time (`POST task[status]=done` → `done` with NULL `completed_at`), web re-stamps on a repeated done while v1 keeps the old stamp, `Task#complete!` is called only from tests. Sibling: projects' `status` + `archived_at` is maintained the same way (`v1/maker/projects_controller.rb:34`).
- (S16 report → S18) v1 task and BOM-line creates default `position` to 0 so API-created rows sort ahead of web rows; `V1::Maker::BomsController#rollup` calls `resolved_target` per line without `includes(:inventory_item, :supplier)`. (→ S15) `projects_controller.rb:440` loads every sheet's full JSON blob (≤1 MB each) only to print `cells.size`. (→ S16) `maker_releases.bom_id`/`repo_node_id` are written but never read in `app/`; on the web `bom_revision` is always 1 — product question. (→ S14) `public/projects/_bom.html.erb:40,50` and `bom_csv.rb:74-75` fall back to `line.part_name` for stock/file lines, which `exactly_one_target` guarantees blank — dead fallbacks.

- (S17 report) **SAFETY NOTE — a destructive switch guarded only by documentation (owner's call, not a simplification).** `DEMO_ACCOUNT_EMAIL` makes WHATEVER row has that address "the demo": `Demo.account` is `User.find_by(email:)` (`demo.rb:37-40`), nothing records that the reset minted the row, `tinkerex:demo:setup` RESETS an existing account at the address rather than refusing it (`tinkerex.rake:57-71`), and the nightly reset deletes every top-level node through the port, destroys and recreates the user with a random password, then purges the trash with a cutoff of now + 1 h (`demo_reset.rb:139-154,183-193`); its refusal looks only at the seed, the port and co-tenants, never at the account or the library's contents. On share/s3 an operator who names their OWN (only) account gets one-click sign-in to it on the front page and loses every library file at 04:00 UTC with their password replaced; a correctly minted demo on a library that already holds real files loses them the second night (the first night's scan indexes them into the demo's catalog). The only guard is the bold sentence at `docs/INSTALL.md:334`, where comparable misconfigurations are refused at boot (`registration.rb`, `mail_settings.rb`). Possible interlocks (each adds behaviour): a `users.demo` flag only `DemoReset.create_account!` sets, required by `Demo.account`, with `setup` refusing an unflagged existing account (the live OVH row marked in the same deploy); and/or `setup` refusing a non-empty library on first mint. Schema half → S23.
- (S17 report → S01) `Registration.validate!` should consume F-S01-1's resolved backend NAME as a `backend:` input (keeping the `REGISTRATION == "open"` short-circuit first), closing the dev-only gap at `registration.rb:47`; `SINGLE_LIBRARY_BACKENDS` restates by name the fact `demo_reset.rb:75` spells by class — one declaration beside S01's reading could serve both. OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19: `SINGLE_LIBRARY_BACKENDS` (`registration.rb:24`: every account indexes one library) and `demo_reset.rb:75` (the reset rebuilds through the port's byte write and empties its trash) are DIFFERENT facts that coincide on {share, s3} — §14b's "agreement across three samples is not identity" applies; keep two declarations, both fed by F-S01-1's resolved name.
- (S17 report → S05) `DemoReset#purge_trash!` calls `TrashPurgeJob`'s PRIVATE reapers via `send` and re-implements `#perform`'s adapter dispatch (`demo_reset.rb:183-193`); the s3 arm is the production path (OVH) and no test exercises it (`demo_reset_test` pins share). A public cutoff-taking entry point on the job would remove both. `recurring.yml:31` ("No-ops unless LIBRARY_BACKEND=share") is stale — `purge_all!` enqueues for s3.
- (S17 report → S04/S18) Account deletion (`registrations_controller.rb:108-118`) destroys the mirror rows and `sync_device_token` but never deprovisions the user's library on the sync server — orphaned for good on chunk_remote. `V1::TestUsersController` mints confirmed accounts outside `Tinkerex::Registration` (kept off share/s3 only by a committed config + hygiene test; the controller itself refuses nothing there).
- (S17 report) Stale prose: `demo.rb:46-47` (POST /demo asks `available?` — it does not), `demo.rb:7-8`, `demo_reset_job.rb:7-9`, `user.rb:121-126`/`registrations_controller.rb:72-76` (a "legacy device-token/v1 bootstrap" that no longer exists); `RESERVED_HANDLES` misses `demo` and `pins`, both mintable by `HANDLE_RE` (defence in depth only; `demo` would collide with `DemoReset::HANDLE`); `User.from_omniauth` never enqueues the first scan (an OIDC-created first account waits ≤15 min).

- (S18 report, CONVERGENT) S18's Opportunity 1 independently re-derived three rules that only the web doors enforce and proposed moving each into the model both doors call: (a) the inventory ledger's delta bounds and project scoping — ABSORBED by F-S19-1 (S18 adds: Postgres overflows an out-of-column delta into an unmapped 500; `on: :create` is required for the validation so the immutability test keeps raising ReadOnlyRecord; a read-side `t.project.user_id == current_user.id` guard is defence in depth for rows already written); (b) the BOM stock-line unit — ABSORBED by F-S16-2 (S18 prefers a reader override `def unit = stock_item&.unit || super` plus carrying the unit in `release_bom_lines`; S16's callback-plus-backfill was weighed against it in audit-2026-10-19-simplification/reports/S16.md — the owner picks the shape; both remove the rule from the door); (c) the task `done`/`completed_at` stamp written three ways across the two task controllers — ABSORBED by the existing X6 / F-40 record (S18 adds the `before_save :stamp_completion` shape and that `Task#complete!` has no production caller). S18's SECURITY NOTE (any device-token holder can post an adjust naming another tenant's `project_id`; the poster's own ledger page then renders that project's name and slug; walking ids lists every tenant's project names) is the same disclosure F-S19-1 records — two independent reviewers reached it by reading; confirm with ONE request and fix on its own track ahead of any simplification work.

Low priority.

- (S26 report, S25/S26) **COVERAGE DEFECT, not a simplification — the CI image renders previews with different tools than production**: CI bakes a native `pdfium-cli` prebuilt (`ci/runner/Dockerfile:165-168`, reported as v0.2.0) while production ships `v0.11.0` WASM (`Dockerfile:116-120`, verified), so the PDF render tests pass against a binary production does not run; F3D is baked in production (`Dockerfile:145-171`) and ABSENT from the CI image (grep verified: only a comment mentions f3d), so every Mesh render test SKIPS in CI while `ci.yml:112-117` celebrates "RAISED COVERAGE"; libvips comes from different distributions; resvg 0.47.0 is pinned three times (`Dockerfile:107`, `ci/runner/Dockerfile:162`, `ci/ensure-resvg.sh`) and Ruby 3.4.7 three times (`.ruby-version`, `Dockerfile:11`, the runner's mise) with nothing checking agreement (`ci/runner/Dockerfile:141-142` claims "the two cannot disagree"). Remedy: fix + a parity guard (a `ci_hygiene` test deriving each renderer pin from the app `Dockerfile` and asserting the runner image matches); the only true single-declaration shape is a `ci-runner` target inside the S25-owned `Dockerfile` taking the renderer stages with `COPY --from` (cross-subsystem and glibc-sensitive).
- (S26 report) Hygiene, not structural: `architecture_invariants_test.rb` carries an unused `SYNC_SIDE_GLOBS` (8 of 9 paths gone) and ~400 comment lines narrating retired constants (987 comment lines vs 291 code lines; its own "READ ORDER WARNING" admits the narration misleads); its prefix-index guard never asserts `conn.tables` is non-empty and `PREFIX_INDEX_EXEMPTIONS` has no stale-entry check; `script/maker_smoke.py` drives `/v1/tree`, `/v1/commit*`, `DELETE /v1/nodes/*` — none routed here (§6; S23 and S26 both found it) and `STORAGE_BACKEND=local` which no code reads — delete or rewrite; `ci.yml:121` still says `docs/STATUS.md` records per-suite figures and `docs_hygiene_test.rb:102`'s STATUS scan omits `.github/`; `ci/runners/setup-*` install FUSE/rclone/mingw for jobs that moved to tinkerex-client (README says the boxes are unregistered); `ci_hygiene_test.rb:207-211,255` and `bin/build-codemirror:47` ("16 DIRECT" vs 17) carry stale prose; `.github/dependabot.yml` cannot see the Docker bases, `Cargo.lock` or the heredoc npm pins. (→ S20) `css_hygiene_test.rb`'s `OURS` list is never cross-checked against `Dir.glob("pages/*.css")` (the "exhaustive list that silently is not" shape — S20 found the same). (→ S25) `deploy_hygiene_test.rb:468` lists an eight-gate set with `test-share`/`test-app`. (→ S27) a Minitest reporter for F-S26-1 would land in `test_helper.rb`.
- (S28 report) **Ownership gap, flagged for a separate small fix**: the spool lives in each CHECKOUT's `tmp/` (`bin/log:605`), and 28 worktrees exist under `.claude/worktrees/`; a record spooled in one worktree is invisible to `bin/log status` everywhere else (including the start-of-run checks in `/finish-branch` and `/deploy`), is deleted with the worktree, and `tmp/` is gitignored so git sees nothing to lose — a default under `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` (falling back to `tmp/`) would make the backlog the repository's. Test asides: the real-spool guard (`log_cli_test.rb:434-451`) uses `git branch --show-current`, which prints "" on CI's detached checkout while `bin/log` writes `detached-<sha>.md`, so in CI it cannot fail (§14e's vacuous shape); the usage test's list omits `close`; `status` counts files not entries. `Unreachable` also covers not-found/misconfig/gh failures, so `note 9999 "x"` exits 3 "unreachable" and spools an unfilable entry (a NotFound outcome would be more honest but changes exit-code semantics `/finish-branch` reads). Stale prose: `.claude/commands/finish-branch.md:28` (the gate list — same as F-S26-1), CLAUDE.md:375 (spool default without "per-checkout"), CLAUDE.md:1350 (`verify-fix-log` presented as general when its only readable input is the frozen 2026-08-19 ledger).
- (S23 report, remaining notes) Perf, not simplification: the tenant-first composite indexes cannot serve association reads that filter by the parent key alone, so without ANALYZE `project.sheets/.connections/.build_log_entries/.tasks/.file_attachments`, `bom.lines`, `inventory_item.transactions` and the supplier/location nullify cascades are full scans of the child table across tenants (small at today's scale; `page_query_budget_test` counts queries, not rows). (→ S16) `task.rb:50-60` documents a reachable polymorphic half-orphan (`taskable_id` with a NULL type via v1) it deliberately does not chase — a both-or-neither normalisation + backfill would close it. (→ S15/S16) the desktop client offers "archived" as a project status and never sends `archived_at` (the X6/F-40 sibling). (→ tinkerex-client) `guessRole` disagrees with `ROLE_BY_EXT` on f3d/fcstd/sldprt and md/txt. S23 adds a stronger reason to KEEP storing `maker_file_attachments.role` (it keeps `Project#cover_node_id` query-free per index card) and suggests one idempotent "re-derive `other` rows from the current `ROLE_BY_EXT`" task instead of a migration-plus-test pair per widening (eight such pairs, ~1,500 lines, exist today — S06's call).

- (S29 report → S08) `gerber.rb:62-67` writes the layer as `layer.<ext>` while `render.mjs:10-12` requires the caller to write each member under its REAL basename because tracespace identifies a layer from its name (KiCad's `*-Edge_Cuts.gbr`/`*-F_Cu.gbr` carry identity in the basename; the viewer keeps it, the thumbnail drops it — it can change the board shape/viewBox an outline layer is drawn in); five copies of one subprocess shape (`stl.rb`, `pdf.rb`, `image_thumb.rb`, `mesh.rb`, `svg_rasterizer.rb`: Tempfile → capture3 → `success? && exist? && size?` → ensure delete → ENOENT → RenderError); three conventions for the `*_BIN` override (constant frozen at load; memo + `reset_bin!`; read per call); `available?` spawns a process on EVERY `GeneratePreviewJob` run, unmemoised; the node subprocess has a wall-clock bound but no heap bound (the recorded 104 s probe ended at the kernel OOM killer, which under `SOLID_QUEUE_IN_PUMA` could as easily choose Puma — `node --max-old-space-size` would keep the failure inside node); `stl.rb:7-10`, `Cargo.toml:5`, `README.md:3-8`, `lib.rs:6-7` still claim the crate "also runs in the Tauri desktop core" — false, the server is the only path. (→ S24) `Dockerfile:98` builds stl-thumbnailer with `cargo build --release`, no `--locked` (a consistent committed lock is honoured, so no live drift, but a manifest/lock mismatch would not fail the build).

- (S27 report) Vacuous or dead test arms in feature rows: `readme_edit_test.rb:240-251` "a versioned backend says nothing" can never run on any leg because setup pins share (`text_file_creation_test.rb:118-138` names this exact trap and fixed its twin with a capabilities stub — S13); `boot_scan_test.rb:27-30` asserts the gate only where `!scannable?` and reduces to `assert_respond_to` on share/s3 (S24); `working_tree_test.rb:78-82`'s `CACHE` name filter is dead in Rails 8.1 (cache hits are flagged `cached: true`; harmless); `settings_hub_test.rb:33-36`'s `other_user!` is passwordless — never signed in today, would silently fail if it were (S20); `test/sqlite_concurrency_test.rb:78` reads `CHUNK_STORE_WORKER` as the worker index, coupling to a stale name in `test_helper.rb:37-51` (MemoryChunkStore and Sync::ChunkReaper are retired). Considered and rejected by S27 with reasons: consolidating the 18 byte-identical `replacing` copies (duplication only, every `remove_method` site removes only a method it created), a shared `count_queries` (the 17 copies count equivalently under `executor_around_test_case`), harness-level platform answers for system tests (would hide the touch path the Linux runners exercise), auto `assert_no_console_errors` in teardown (unmeasured blast radius), harness snapshots of ENV/`Rails.cache` (no recorded leak). `test/system/README.md` counts and its `rm -rf tmp/dev-chunks*` advice are stale.

- (S12 report → S11) `docs/invariants/addons.md:470-482` says the consumer of `code: "invalid_params"` "IS NOW A BUNDLE", but the OpenSCAD bundle explicitly does not branch on it (`tinkerex-addons openscad/sidecar.js:54-61`), and the generator template tells new authors "A bundle branches on `code`" (`README.md.tt:10-11`, `app.py.tt:25-27`) — the docs contradict the only consumer; `addons_controller.rb:1274` lists `image/png` in `PROXIED_INLINE_TYPES` while `:1250-1260` claims the list is "THE UNION OF WHAT THE SIDECARS ACTUALLY EMIT" (no sidecar emits PNG); `addons_controller.rb:255-256`: a `Net::ReadTimeout` becomes `ServiceProxy::Error` → `head :not_found`, so a slow OpenSCAD render reaches the bundle as a body-less 404, the same answer as an unconfigured sidecar. (→ S25) `deploy_hygiene_test.rb:378,455` glob `sidecar/*/Dockerfile` directly instead of asking `Tinkerex::Sidecars#names` — the same discovery written twice. CARRIED FORWARD from the 2026-08-19 audit: F-76 (the boxes.py commit pinned in both `sidecar/boxes/Dockerfile:20` and `requirements.txt:5`; README tells a forker to change only the latter) is still unlanded at HEAD and still applies verbatim — not re-raised to avoid a duplicate. The render-deadline chain (`OPENSCAD_TIMEOUT` default 60 < gunicorn `--timeout 120` ≤ manifest `service_timeouts: 90`; `params_for` hard-codes 30) breaks "clean 422, not a worker crash" if the env knob is raised — a one-line fix, not structural. ## 5. Duplicates and superseded findings

- S10's cross-note "the ready-and-attached preview predicate repeats ~10x across private and public views" → absorbed by F-S08-2 (authoritative: S08). Not a separate finding.

- **F-S03-1 ⟷ F-S02-1 are two halves of ONE cross-adapter design ("the trash entry model") and must land as one slice.** F-S03-1 owns the ENTRY-KEY SHAPE on S3 (root-relative keys, markers in the object set, one subtree walk for delete and undelete, live rows only) and stands. Its undelete MEMBERSHIP rule as written ("a node revives if its object came back, or is a directory on the path to one") is SUPERSEDED by F-S02-1's recorded membership (`trash_root_node_id`): existence-at-the-rebuilt-key is the same inference `Share#revive_subtree!` makes today and reproduces defect (b) (two same-named rows, one a purged older trash tombstone, both "come back"). Both reviewers independently traced the same root cause (`s3.rb:530-532`'s false assumption that scan-tombstoned descendants have NULL paths). Authoritative owner for the membership representation: S02; for the S3 key shape and marker handling: S03. OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19 — SUPERSEDED TOO: F-S03-1's tombstone set (audit-2026-10-19-simplification/reports/S03.md:94 keeps `Subtree.node_ids` for the S3 cascade's `update_all`, `s3.rb:568-569`); both adapters follow F-S02-1's live-rows-only cascade. "ONE SLICE" REFINED to two consistent slices: (A) no migration — F-S03-1 step 1 with a live-rows-only object set and tombstone UPDATE, plus F-S02-1's `.present` cascade on Share; (B) migration — `trash_root_node_id` on both adapters plus F-S03-1 step 2 (the S3 folder restore reading the column). Step 2 never ships before (B); the blank-ETag hardening ticket (ex F-S03-2) lands before step 2.
- OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19 corrects this bullet's first clause: S05 (S05.md:123) and S23 (S23.md:346) rejected an explicit tombstone-KIND column; S03 rejected "an explicit tombstone-kind or `trash_root_id` column" BY NAME (S03.md:205), arguing root-relative keys make the entry's LOCATION derivable — which does not answer MEMBERSHIP, so F-S02-1's column stands over it. Original text: S05 and S03 each REJECTED "an explicit tombstone-KIND column (scan vs trash)" because the NULL `storage_path` is the mechanism that disarms revive-by-path. F-S02-1's `trash_root_node_id` is a DIFFERENT fact (which entry holds the bytes), not a kind flag, and keeps the NULL path; the residual duplication ("non-NULL ⇒ trash") is to be pinned by a guard test. Not a contradiction, recorded so the three rows read consistently.

- S14's Opportunity 2 CO-SIGNS F-S10-2 (the public half of the shared `Viewer`) after checking the claims independently; counted ONCE under F-S10-2 (authoritative: S10, with S14 owning `public/files_controller.rb#view` and `public/files/view.html.erb`). One refinement from S14: the zip-archive arm should drop a nil URL (`.compact`/`filter_map`) like the other two layer arms so files-preview.md:222-227's "absent layer on both arms or on neither" holds for all three.
- S15's pointers to S14 (`#thumbs` backfilling unpainted ids; `#hidden_*`/`#flatten_dirs` re-walking; `#repo_cards` repeating #show's loop): the first is absorbed into F-S14-1; the second is not material (in-memory over ≤ DIR_CAP dirs — the capped BASIS is the real problem, fixed by F-S14-1); the third is ~3 lines around one implementation, pinned on both sides — rejected as line-count only.

- The convergent S01/S06 cross-cutting pattern "chunk_remote has no discover → file → catalogue SEQUENCE" is RESOLVED into F-S07-1 (positional claims), which the S07 reviewer showed is strictly better than a sequenced pipeline: the cap hole exists inside ScanJob's own sequence, and driving a pipeline from the 2-minute pull multiplies the catalog's per-tick cost by 7.5. After F-S07-1, ScanJob's post-reconcile order is a LATENCY choice for the model catalog, not a correctness one (project intake's own ordering needs — `project-intake.md:134-146` — are S06's and unchanged). S24's schedule-representation review may add a latency-only note; it does not reopen the correctness question.

- S18's Opportunity 1 (three web-only rules) is counted ONCE under F-S19-1 (ledger), F-S16-2 (unit) and the pre-existing X6/F-40 record (task stamp); S18 is credited as an independent co-sign. S18's security note = F-S19-1's cross-tenant disclosure.
- S22's "shared lifecycle module for the three viewers" was considered and REJECTED by S22 itself in favour of per-controller fixes (F-S09-1, F-S22-1) plus ONE shared Turbo-Back test helper; the coordinator agrees — a shared runtime module would move three differently-shaped expensive steps behind one type.

- S24's Opportunity 1 (the sequenced sweep) and the S01/S06 cross-cutting pattern it answers are reconciled with F-S07-1 under F-S24-1's "RELATION TO F-S07-1" note: F-S07-1 = correctness (positional claims, closes the cap hole); F-S24-1 = representation + latency (one declared sequence replaces five spellings). F-S24-1 DEMOTED 2026-10-19 by the materiality adjudication (its correctness motive is what F-S07-1 removes; the consolidation adds a cross-job concurrency group) — F-S07-1 alone lands; the sweep job is a latency note in §4.
- S23's "Derive `maker_file_attachments.role` instead of storing it" was considered and rejected by S23 and, independently, by S06 (the v1 API permits a client-set role; `EntryPhotosController` writes "image") — recorded once.

- F-S29-2 and F-S26-2 both remove `ci/ensure-resvg.sh`'s two `ci.yml` steps; F-S26-2 owns the WORKFLOW half (gate jobs carry plumbing only) and F-S29-2 owns the TOOLCHAIN half (delete the script, `--locked` on both Dockerfiles, the pin-equality assertion, the CI presence gate). The renderer-parity DEFECT recorded under S26 (pdfium native vs WASM, F3D absent from CI, Ruby pinned thrice) is the same drift class; F-S29-2's hygiene + presence tests are the guard shape its remedy needs — land them together. OVERLAP PASS 2026-10-19: order — F-S29-2's pin-equality assertion lands before the resvg steps leave `ci.yml`; F-S29-2's scope no longer lists those lines (its CI-only presence gate is deferred to the renderer-parity decision).

## 6. Final priorities and dependencies

Written 2026-10-19 by the coordinator after all five audit-the-audit passes landed (coverage, overlap, materiality, schema, priority — reports/AUDIT-*.md) and were adjudicated. The priority pass's scoring rule was adopted and re-applied after the materiality adjudication; where the two passes disagreed, the decision and its reason are in the entry's adjudication line. **Nothing in this audit was executed**: no test, gate, request, SQL plan (beyond the two in-memory EXPLAINs recorded under F-S05-1) or browser. Every "reachable", "red today" and "goes red under mutation" claim is read from code at 75124d67, and each slice below begins by driving the probe that confirms its defect.

### 6.1 Scoring rule (the priority pass's, applied uniformly)
Score = Impact (1–5: 5 = reachable data loss/corruption, tenancy breach, or a deploy gate that can ship an empty secret; 4 = a reachable user-visible wrong/stuck result, 500 or silent wrong write, or a missing gate over the repo's own definition of green; 3 = latent — needs an unscheduled path, crafted request, operator mistake or rare race — or a duplicated decision that has already drifted into a recorded bug; 2 = duplication/dead state with no recorded drift; 1 = cosmetic) + Confidence (high +1 · med-high +0.5 · medium 0 · med-low −0.5) + Effort (S 0 · M −1 · L −2) + Unlocks (+1 if a hard prerequisite of another finding or a guard protecting 3+) + Gated (−1 if an owner decision or live-data measurement must precede the code). Tiers: P1 ≥ 5.0 · P2 4.0–4.5 · P3 3.0–3.5 · P4 ≤ 2.5. A finding inside a same-PR group is scheduled at the group's tier.

### 6.2 Final tiers — 46 ranked findings (+ 8 demoted to tickets, §4)
**P1 — do first (14).** Guards that make "green" mean §15, then reachable data/tenancy/wrong-answer defects whose fix IS the representation change:
1. F-S26-1 `bin/ci` compares the three legs' run counts (GG 4; unlocks every later slice; fixes the close-out file's wrong instruction). Prereq: none. New `Ci::LegRuns` under Zeitwerk.
2. F-S27-1 the harness owns the `FileLayer.current` memo's per-test lifetime (GG 3; protects every slice that adds tests). Before F-S27-2.
3. F-S27-2 step 1 the verifying web sign-in helper + `PublicFixture#sign_in_as` + the three vacuous tests (GG 3). The ~100-file sweep is its own later change.
4. F-S03-1 one S3 trash-entry shape — step 1 (the delete side) first, no migration (DATA 5 on the s3 deployment and OVH). T1 with F-S02-1's cascade; step 2 only in T2.
5. F-S02-1 `nodes.trash_root_node_id` records trash membership (DATA 5 on share, the real-data deployment). T1 = the `.present` cascade (no migration); T2 = the column + both undeletes + F-S03-1 step 2 + the mutation contract's folder delete/restore case.
6. F-S19-1 `InventoryTransaction` owns what a postable ledger row is (SEC/DATA 5: cross-tenant project-name disclosure via any device token; non-finite rows in an immutable ledger). Security track, minimal, first.
7. F-S07-1 the catalogue honours discovery's positional claim (RD 4: permanent wrong auto models past the 50-folder cap; the chunk_remote order race). Hard prerequisite of F-S07-2.
8. F-S08-1 one "does this row need a job?" predicate (RD 4: wedged previews never heal from any read door). Hard prerequisite of F-S08-2.
9. F-S10-1 (narrowed) rescue OUTSIDE each cache `fetch` + GerberArchive's banner arm + namespace bumps (RD 4: a 30-day cached nil an anonymous visitor can plant). The shared door is optional.
10. F-S09-1 the Measure view's phase has one owner (RD 4: dead Turbo-Back snapshot; double load). Shares the Turbo-Back helper with F-S22-1.
11. F-S22-1 the 3D viewer's lifecycle on the Gerber model (RD 4). One PR with F-S09-1 recommended.
12. F-S15-2 "the browser draft is the whole truth" inside `merge(:draft)` (RD 4: Apply resurrects deleted blocks/tabs; Save refuses valid drafts). Needs the owner's yes on spec §10.9.
13. F-S15-1 one editing-aware "subfolders with no tiles" answer (RD 4: repo images never backfilled, lasting on chunk_remote).
14. F-S25-1 one secret-site walk shared by preflight and test (GG/OPS 4: the deploy-gating copy is untested). Before F-S12-2 (same file).

**P2 (17).** F-S01-1 one reading of `LIBRARY_BACKEND` (LD/OPS 3, patched twice; unlocks F-S17-1/F-S18-1 and S25's key set; CLAUDE.md edit under the ceiling) · F-S16-1 sheet autosave decided by comparison (RD/DATA 4; owner sub-choice: persist or disable sorts/drags) · F-S14-1 one "shown" answer for a visitor's page (RD 4 on anonymous pages; the /u/:handle card change last, with the owner's yes) · F-S11-1 stamp each addon file with its revision (RD 4; additive migration; after F-X-1, same file) · F-S23-1 `NOT_EDGES` completeness rule (GG 3; land BEFORE T2, F-S11-1 and F-S16-2 add columns) · F-S04-1 (narrowed) explicit `version:`/`manifest:` at both `upsert` sites (LD 3; gates the restored-server cursor fix and upstream prune scheduling) · F-S13-2 `CommitPaths` reads each path once (LD race 3) · F-S28-2 `bin/log` filing helper (RD 3 during an outage) · F-S21-2 (narrowed) the batch refuses `NotFound` per file + the build-log door's rescue + one stat-then-enqueue (RD 4 on share/s3), with F-S21-1 retiring the dead project-target arm inside the same PR · F-S28-1 delete `bin/migrate-record` (OPS 3; trivial) · F-S12-2 one owner of the catalogue admission rules (GG 3; after F-S25-1) · F-S06-1 `ProjectIntake::Result` as one row per file (DUP 3, drifted once) · F-S13-1 the commit box computes each row once (DUP 3, drifted once) · **F-S33-1** stored roles follow the vocabulary by one sweep rule (RD 4: electronics heroes wrong for Gerbers attached 2026-07-31 → 2026-09-06; retires the per-widening migration) · **F-S34-1** a spinner turns only inside a poll cell (RD 4 on public build-log/image-block thumbnails and both pickers; trivial) · **F-S34-2** `[hidden]` authoritative once in `@layer base` (RD 4: the YAML drawer renders while hidden; closes a four-times-recurring bug class; co-owned with S20).

HARD: F-S26-1 → every later slice (the gate) · F-S27-1 → F-S27-2 (same file) · F-S07-1 → F-S07-2 · F-S08-1 → F-S08-2 · T1 (F-S03-1 step 1 + F-S02-1's cascade) → T2 (the column + undeletes + F-S03-1 step 2) · F-S23-1 → T2, F-S11-1, F-S16-2 (new columns must be drawn) · the blank-ETag hardening ticket (ex F-S03-2) → T2 · F-S29-2's pin assertion → the §4 renderer-parity fix · the two `ci.yml` resvg steps leave (F-S29-2's own PR, or F-S26-2) → `git rm ci/ensure-resvg.sh` · F-S04-1 → the restored-server cursor fix and any upstream `prune_changelog` scheduling · F-S01-1 → F-S17-1 (optional input), F-S18-1 (producer moves to boot), S25's key set, `Registration` (pass the resolved name IN; never a raising reader from a second initializer) · F-S05-1 → F-S05-2 · F-S21-1 → F-S21-2 (one PR) · F-S19-1 → F-S16-2 (same model) · F-X-1 → F-S11-1 (same file) · F-S25-1 → F-S12-2 (same test file) · F-S34-1 and F-S34-2 → one S34 PR with S20 (both edit `components.css`; F-S34-2 also edits `shell.css`, which F-S20-1 rewrites — coordinate). SOFT: F-S27-1/F-S27-2 step 1 → every slice adding integration tests (they get the reset and the verifying sign-in by construction; F-S19-1's test (c) in particular asserts 200 first) · F-S10-1 → F-S10-2 (preference only; no shared file) · F-S09-1 ⟷ F-S22-1 (shared Turbo-Back helper; one PR recommended) · F-S08-1/2 → the F-S23-2 index ticket. MUST NOT LAND TOGETHER: F-S07-1 + F-S07-2 · F-S08-1 + F-S08-2 · F-S19-1 + F-S16-2 · T2 + F-S02-2 · F-S10-2 + F-S08-2 (both rewrite the two file pages and controllers) · F-S27-2's sweep + any feature slice · F-S29-2's `--locked` half + any gate-only slice · F-S12-1's two sidecar halves (one image per `bin/publish-sidecar` run).

### 6.4 Shared constraints every slice meets
- **CLAUDE.md is at its ceiling: 1,360 lines = `MAX_LINES`** (`test/docs_hygiene_test.rb:71`). Eight findings edit it (F-S26-1 §13/§15, F-S01-1 and F-X-1 §14a, T2's two-tombstone bullet, F-S10-1 §14d's setter list, F-S23-1/F-S23-2 §5, F-S24-2 §4/§14e). Each must be net ≤ 0 lines; S-1 LOWERS the constant as the ratchet comment asks; merge CLAUDE.md-editing PRs one at a time (they all touch one constant). The §6 endpoint-count correction (§4) is net-zero too.
- **Three-leg run counts are compared by hand until F-S26-1 merges**; `.claude/commands/finish-branch.md:28-30` today names a non-existent `test-share` gate and asks for equal ASSERTION counts, which never hold (`tinkerex#131`'s ±2 flap is the known noise).
- **Parallel local `bin/ci` runs collide**: the chunk_remote leg kills whatever listens on `SYNC_PORT` (3002; `config/ci.rb:149-157`) — give each worktree its own port and set `TINKEREX_SYNC_DIR`.
- **Generated files conflict on every rebase**: `db/schema.rb`'s version line and `docs/schema-map.html` — regenerate after rebasing, never hand-merge.
- **CI capacity** is four self-hosted Linux runners (CLAUDE.md §15, a dated measurement): five parallel PRs × seven jobs queue; stagger pushes.
- **Bytes never through Rails, the port's parity across share/s3/chunk_remote, and mutation-checked guards** (§15) apply to every slice; T1/T2 in particular assert the S3 WIRE key shape in FakeS3 (§14e's inverse-encoding lesson).

### 6.5 Best first slices (each one PR under §15; the five touch no common file and can open in parallel)
- **S-1 Suite honesty (wave 0):** F-S26-1 → F-S27-1 → F-S27-2 step 1. Files: `config/ci.rb`, new `lib/ci/leg_runs.rb` + test, `test/test_helper.rb`, `test/support/public_fixture.rb`, the six pin lines in two job tests, `test/system/README.md`, CLAUDE.md + `docs_hygiene_test.rb` (lower the ceiling), `.claude/commands/{finish-branch,execute-plan}.md`. Proof: LegRuns unit tests with their mutations; once end-to-end, `TEST=test/models` on `test-s3` goes red naming both counts, and a planted failure under `test` goes red (tee swallows nothing); the harness probe (a directly assigned ChunkRemote is gone after teardown; delete the reset → red, and `fresh_share_library_cleanup_test.rb:81-89` with it); `sign_in_web!` refuses an unconfirmed user (drop the assertion → red); a wrong password turns the two vacuous suites red at the helper. Why first: no product code, no deploy; every later slice's green then means §15; hidden order dependence surfaces once, centrally. Risk: tests that pass today only via a leaked adapter or an anonymous session — if more than a handful go red, split F-S27-1 out. Owners: S26, S27, S14 (`public_fixture.rb`), S06/S07 (job tests), S30 (prose).
- **S-2 Trash, delete side (T1):** F-S03-1 step 1 + F-S02-1's no-migration cascade. In `S3#delete_directory!`: the object set = `.present` file rows + each directory's marker (HEAD-checked), root-relative destination keys from the parent-edge walk, markers deleted in phase 2, the phase-3 UPDATE over `.present` ids (`s3.rb:532,545-546,568`); Share's cascade over `.present` ids (`share.rb:423`); `Share#revive_subtree!` skips rows that kept a path or own a `.trash/<id>` entry (`:694-712`); correct the false comments (`s3.rb:530-531`) and the mislabelled fixture (`subtree_test.rb:41-48`); rider: `Share#write`'s `mkdir_p` outside `begin` (§4). Proof: new s3 cases (a folder holding a scan-tombstoned file still deletes — drop `.present` → red; a deleted folder stays deleted across a scan — drop the markers → red; a nested entry is root-relative), new share cases (a NAS-deleted file inside a later-deleted folder is never offered for restore; a folder delete does not re-stamp an older tombstone — separate the times with `travel_to(3.days.ago)`, never `.to_i`; a restore after a replace does not 500 while the earlier entry exists — drop the skip → red); existing delete/undelete/cap suites, `adapter_mutation_contract.rb`, `share_trash_test`, `trash_purge_job_test`. Why first: live data-facing defects on BOTH deployed writable backends, no migration, no interface change; it also stops leftover markers re-minting deleted app-made folders (incl. OVH's nightly demo reset through `S3#delete`); T2's prerequisite. Owners: S03, S02, S01 (do NOT narrow `Subtree.node_ids`), S05, S17.
- **S-3 Ledger integrity and tenancy:** F-S19-1 alone. Step 0: write the v1 probes and watch them fail at HEAD (`%w[Infinity -Infinity NaN 1e400 0]` answer 201; an adjust naming another user's project stores it). Then: `MAX_DELTA` + `numericality: { other_than: 0, in: -MAX_DELTA..MAX_DELTA }` ON CREATE; `apply!` scopes `project_id` to the item owner's projects and normalises `note`; the web door loses `parse_delta`/`MAX_DELTA`/the REASONS re-check/the project lookup and rescues `RecordInvalid`; `Projects::BomLinesController#consume` rescues `RecordInvalid`; a one-line read guard at `inventory_items/show.html.erb:88` so already-written rows stop disclosing. Proof: model tests for the five strings (drop `in:` → red; drop `other_than: 0` → red); v1 422 with the ledger unchanged; v1 cross-tenant → 201 with a stored nil, then the poster's item page asserted 200 FIRST and free of the foreign name (drop `user_id: locked.user_id` → red; drop the view guard with a pre-planted foreign row → red). Owners: S19, S18 (v1: a zero delta now 422s — check tinkerex-client never sends one), S16.
- **S-4 Preview reliability:** F-S08-1 + F-S10-1 (narrowed, in-place). F-S10-1: rescue outside each `fetch`, GerberArchive's banner arm, both namespaces bumped (the `ContentRead` door only if review wants it). F-S08-1: `Enqueue.needs_job?(retry_failed:)` asked by the doors that already hold a row; `MissingBackfill` loads the batch's rows; name the in-flight set once (ready for F-S08-2); correct `enqueue.rb:43-67` (incl. the caller list) and `measure-view.md:320-333`. Proof: under a MemoryStore, FetchError → nil + banner then the real answer with a second read counted (put the rescue back inside the fetch → red; delete the banner line → red); request tests with a `processing` row stamped 3 days ago via `travel_to` — the Files page and file page re-enqueue it and the backfill counts it, a fresh `processing` row is not re-enqueued, the inspector's "failed is never re-enqueued" stays green (delete the stall line → red; drop `retry_failed: false` at `current_thumbnail` → red). Files disjoint from the other four slices (a trivial textual neighbour with the later upload slice at `files_controller.rb:1230/1244`). Owners: S10, S08, S14, S09, S06.
- **S-5 Catalogue claims:** F-S07-1 alone. `ProjectDiscovery.positional_claims(user)` public and uncapped, `adoptable` defined through it, `ModelIntake.claimed_folders` includes it; re-point DiscoveryOrderTest's swallow assertion; prose in `model-intake.md`, `project-intake.md`, `scan_job.rb`, `recurring.yml`. Proof: a folder discovery will adopt is not catalogued before adoption (drop the union → red); a dot-folder under `Projects/` is still catalogued (replace the reader with a path test → red); a capped discovery (`cap: 1`) leaves no auto model for the rest (cap the reader → red); the same through `Library::ScanJob` with `PROJECT_DISCOVERY_MAX=1`; `model_intake_defaults_test.rb:51,80-81` change on purpose.
