### 6.6 Defects to fix independently of any simplification (from §4; the priority pass's ranking, coordinator-checked)
Highest first: (1) `FilesController#restore` (`files_controller.rb:748-793`) compares the restored blob against the CURRENT disk bytes only to refuse a no-op, then writes with `replace: true` — a pending uncommitted edit is silently replaced and is not recoverable by revert (revert returns to HEAD); compare against HEAD's blob per git-editing.md's own rule and refuse or confirm (DATA, one door; coordinator-verified at HEAD). (2) The demo-reset destructive switch (S17 safety note) — owner decision, decided with F-S17-1. (3) `CHUNK_LOCAL_DIR` under the mounted volume pinned by no suite (GG over a data-destroying misconfiguration; rides F-S25-1's file). (4) `production.rb:178` defaults `ADDON_SOURCE_PATH` to a nonexistent `public/addons`, so OVH's `/tools` says "configured but returned nothing" — a one-line quick win. (5) Zero-byte desktop-synced files unreadable on chunk_remote (ride F-S04-1). (6) The cursor-ahead-of-a-restored-sync-server gap (AFTER F-S04-1, never before — it routes into the broken resync arm). (7) `ChunkRemote#finalize_upload` `info: nil` on a conflict (no preview, intake or notice). (8) `readmes_controller` without a `NotFound` arm 500s inside the mirror lag. (9) `EntryPhotosController#destroy` finds any of the user's attachments, then 500s — scope the find. (10) `gerber.rb` writes layers as `layer.<ext>`, dropping the basename that identifies the layer. (11) the uploader and importer navigate over their own failure message. (12) a `geometry` build for any extension stores a PNG as `geometry` — a door-side guard, after F-S08-1 (S33 assessed). (13) the LightBurn `i`-flag mismatch (needs a FORMAT_REVISION bump). (14) the S3 listing vs concurrent web delete ghost node — probe with FakeS3 first, after T1 (S05 owns). (15) the per-checkout spool (rides F-S28-2). (16) `DemoReset#purge_trash!`'s s3 arm untested — before or with T1. (17) the image pickers mount a poller with no cells (S34 → S15; rides F-S34-1). (18) the one-liners: `rescan` drops `q`/`selected`; `relocate` 500s on `:in_working_tree`; `chosen_parent_node_id` lacks its `is_a?(String)` check; the editor parses JSON before `res.ok`; `RESERVED_HANDLES` misses `demo`/`pins`; `model_owning` ignores `dismissed_at`; the prefix-index guard never asserts its table set is non-empty; the template-hygiene sweep never renders the available-addon branches (set `BOXES_SIDECAR_URL`); the 52-class addon contract is unguarded and 39 classes stale (S32/S34 convergent). Sibling repos: the desktop client sends role `"reference"` (422) and posts a fourth role vocabulary; tinkerex-addons' per-key drift comparison and the duplicated plan maths.

### 6.7 Owner decisions that gate ranked work
Spec §10.9 (F-S15-2) · reopen `model-intake.md:177-192` with a measured would-delete count (F-S07-2's full design) · sheet sorts/drags: persist or disable (F-S16-1) · BOM unit shape: derived (recommended) or stored (F-S16-2) · a hidden panel in edit mode (F-S15-1) and the /u/:handle card (F-S14-1) · the demo on chunk_remote and the destructive switch (F-S17-1) · filing defaults going live for untouched users (F-S33-2) · CI rendering with production's tools (the parity gap; decides F-S29-2's presence gate) · version-pinned attachments (the F-S23-2 column ticket).

### 6.8 What this audit did not verify
Nothing was executed; the sibling repositories were read at their local, unfetched checkouts (tinkerex-sync b849e08, tinkerex-addons d3ab6fd, tinkerex-client 399ccdb); no live deployment, data volume, browser, SeaweedFS bucket, CI runner image or GitHub Actions history was inspected; effort is estimated from scope text. The 2026-09-07 usage probe (memory: "project layer unused") was NOT used to discount the project-page, BOM or sheet findings, because the October redesign may have changed usage. Each slice's first step is the probe that turns these readings into observations.
